Since the issue involved in all these apPeals is one and the
same, all these appeals are being hearrl and disposed of by way of
this common judgment.
2.HeardSriV.ManoharRao,SriN.PraveenReddy,
Sri Ravula Shankar Reddy and Sri ? Venkateshwar Reddy,
learned counsel for the appellants-claimants and the learned
Government Pleader for Appeals appearing for the respondent'
3. Learped counsel for the appellants had contended that the
lands along with structures belonging to the appellants were
acquired by the Government for the purpose of irrigation projects'
Learned counsel further contended that the State Government has

L.A.A.S.No.29 of 2025 & batch

Land A,:quisition Officer for acquisition of the house sites and
structurr:s for Srisailam Project, and a Division Bench of this Court
in L.A.A.S.No.4 of 2016 has taken a decision that the said G.O.
applies to all other irrigation Proiects also and accordingly,
allowed L.A.A.s.No.4 of 2076 aide iudgment, dated 15.09.2022,
enhancirrg the market value by two mo.re times (1+2) than the
value fbred by the Land Acquisition Officer with all consequential
benefits as stipulated under the amended Land Acquisition Act.
4. Learned counsel for the appellants had further contended
that fotl,rwing the said judgment, dated 15.09.2022, the very same
DivisiorL Bench has disposed of L.A.A:S.No.125 of 2015 and batctU
aide conrmon judgment, dated 27.09.2022. Aggrieved by the said
conunorr judgment, dated 27.09.2022, the State has carried the
matter to the Honourable Supreme Court by filing
S.L.P.(Civil).No.28925 of 2024 and the same was dismissed by the
Honourable Supreme Court uide order, dated 17.09.2024,

present appeals also deserve to be disposed of by enhancing the

5. Further, learned counsel for the appellants had fairly
conceded that the appeals are filed with delay of nearly 4000 to
5000 d.ays, as admittedly, the appellants are Poor farmers and
illiterates, and therefore, interest may be denied for the delay
period in preferring the appeals.
6. Learned Government Pleader apPearing for the respondent
did not dispute the same.
7. In the present cases, the lands were acquired for the purpose
of Rangasamudram Balancing Reservoir and Priyadarshini ]urala

Project. Admittedly, except for the delay, the rest of the issues in
the prese)nt appeals are squarely covered by the judgment, dated
15.09.2022, rendered in L.A.A.S.No.4 of 20'I..6 and also the common
judgment, dated 27.09.2022, rendered in L.A.A.S.No.125 of 2015
and batch, which was confirmed by the Honourable Supreme

be disposed of in terms of the aforesaid judgments, dated
15.09.2022 and 27.09.2022,by enhancing the market value by two
more tinres (1+2) than the value fixed by the Land Acquisition
Officer

L.A.A.S.Nos.125. 144.27O and 356 of 2O15.62 and 68 of 2O21
COMMON JUDGMENT : (per Justice G. Sri Devi)
The hs in this batch of Appeals preferred by the claimants
pertain to common acquisition proceedings and therefore, they
are heard together and being disposed of by this common
judgment.
2. Heard learned counsel for the claimants and Sri D.Kiran,
learned Assistant Government Pleader for the respondent.
Perused the material available on record.
3. The present acquisition was for the purpose of submergence
of Priyadarshini Joorala Project/ancillary projects under
Priyadarshini Joorala Project. This Court uide judgment, dated
L5.O9.2O22 has already elaborately analysed the evidence and
considered the identical issue raised in these appeals in
L.A.A.S.No.4 of 2016. Both the counsel have not disputed the
factum of commonality of the evidence and the issue in these
appeals and in L.A.A.S.No.4 of 2[l6,wherein this Court has
allowed the appeal enhancing the market value by two more

2
times (,.'..+21 than the value fixed by the L.A.O. with all other
consequential benefits as stipulated under the amended Act.
4. Therefore, following the judgment passed by this Court in
L.A.A.S.No.4 of 2016, dated 15.09.2022 and for the reasons
'stated therein, all these appeals stand disposed of by enhancing
the market value of the acquired land by two more times (l+21
than the value fixed by the L.A.O. with all other consequential
benefits as stipulated under the amended Act. No order as to
costs
Miscellaneous petitions, if any, shall stand closed.
JUSTICE G. SRI DEVI
JUSTICE M.G. PRIYADARSINI

L.A.A.S.No.4 of 2O16
COMMON JUDGMENT : (per Justice G. Sri Devi)
.
This appeal is preferred by the claimants seeking enhancement
of compensation awarded by the Senior Civil Judge at Narayanpet
(hereinafter referred to as "the reference Court") in L.A.O.P.No.47 of
2OL2, dated 19.03.2013. By the impugned order, the reference Court
has awarded the market value for the houses/structures of the
claimants one time more (1+1) than what was awarded by the Land
Acquisition Officer in Award No.S+ of 2O1 1, dated 10.11 .2OLL-2. The houses and structures of the claimants situated in
Anugonda Village, were acquired by the Government for the purpose of
submergence under Priyadarshini Joorala Project, by issuing draft
notification under Section a (1) of the Land Acquisition Act, 1894 (for
short "the Act") on O9.O9.2OO9. After due enquiry, the Land
Acquisition Officer has passed an award. on 10.11 .2OlL fixing
compensation for houses, structures and open places on the basis of
prevailing rates as on the date of notification relying upon the
valuation lixed by the requisition authority, Irrigation Department.
The claimants having reieived the compensation under protest, sought
for reference to the civil Court under Section 18 of the Act, seeking

2
GSD,J&MGP,J
Laas 4 2016
enhancernent of compensation. By the impugned order, the reference
Court has enhanced the market value one time more (1+1) than what
was awerded by the Land Acquisition Officer. Seeking further
enhancernent of compensation, the claimants preferred the present
appeal.
3. He,rrd the learned counsel for the appellantsfclaimants and Sri
D.Kiran, learned Assistant Government Pleader for Appeals. Perused
the mater:ial available on record.
4. Thr: main contention of the learned counsel for the claimants is
that even under the Government Orders i.e., G.O.Ms.No.234, dated
15.10. 1993, marked as Ex.A6, the Government has passed orders
granting compensatton 7+2 times for the houses/structures acquired
in respect of Srisailam Project. It is submitted that inasmuch as the
present acquisition of the houses/structures of the claimants also for
the purpose of irrigation project i.e., Priyadarshini Joorala Project,
Ex.A6 is r:qually applies to the case of the claimants and therefore, the
learned counsel seeks to enhance the market value of the
compenszrtion 1+2 times than what was awarded by the L.A.O.
5. The, Assistant Gpvernment Pleader for Appeals would submit
that the i.O. covered by Ex.A6 was issued in respect of Srisailam
project and whereas the present acquisition pertains to Priyadarshini
t
s-!

3
GSD,J&MGP,J
Laas 4 2016
Joorala Project, for which no specific G.O. was issued by the
Government and therefore, Ex.A6 cannot be taken into consideration.
He further submits that as seen from Ex.Al, the reference Court, in
respect of earlier acquisition in O.P.No.611 of L999, has enhanced the
market value by one time more than the value fixed by the L.A.O. As
seen from Ex.A2, this Court in A.S.No.3375 of 2OOO has confirmed the
market value fixed in Ex.Al. Even the Apex Court, as seen from
Ex.A3, has dismissed the appeal confirming the fixation of the said
market value by the reference Court. Therefore, the learned Assistant
Government Pleader for Appeals, relying on Ex.A3, submits that the
market value fixed by the reference Court is based on evidence and
needs no interference by this Court.
6. A perusal of the record discloses that in respect of the earlier
acquisition proceedings pertaining to Gaddampally H/o. Anugonda
Village, the reference Court has passed orders in O.P.No.29 of 2OO4
and Batch, dated 23.OL.2OO6 enhancing the market value by one time
more than thp value fixed by the L.A.O. The said order was marked as
Ex.A10. Inasmuch as the present acquisition proceedings relates to
the adjacent village covered by Ex.A10, the reference Court relying on
Ex.AlO has fixed the .rnarket value by one time more than the value
fixed by the L.A.O. The record further discloses that the Government
has issued orders on 15.10.1993, which was marked as Ex.A6,

4
GSD,J&MGP,J
Laas 4 2016
ordering firr enhancement of compensation two times more (1+2) than
the value tixed by the L.A.O., in respect of the acquisition of structures
that were acquired for the purpose of Srisailam Project. Although the
learned Assistant Government Pleader submits that this project is
entirely different one and the said G.O. covered by Ex.A6 which was
issued for Srisailam project cannot be applied in the absence of any
specific G.O. issued for the present project, this Court is not inclined to
accept the said contention for the reason that the present acquisition
proceedings also pertains to Irrigation purpose. Even the other
contentior:. of the learned Assistant Government Pleader that under
Ex.A3, tht: Apex Court has confirmed the earlier fixation of market
value by r,:ference Court by one more time (1+1) than the one fixed by
the L.A.O. and therefore, the present enhancement made by the
reference ,lourt which is in tune with Ex.A3 cannot be disturbed is
also unsustainable for the reason that Ex.A6 issued by the
Governme:rt granting l+2 has not been brought to the notice of the
Apex Court.
7 . It is relevant to refer clause (ii) of Ex.A6, which specifies t!;rat "the
market ualue of the structures inctuding well which has been treated as
structure L,g the Land. Abquisition Officer in his award.; and. house sites
shall be firced. at three times the ualue (1+2) d.eterrnined. bg tlrc Land.
Acquisition Officers." Even though Ex.A6 pertains to the acquisition

5
GSD,J&MGP,J
Laas 4 2016
proceedings in respect of Srisailam Project, the benefit thereof cannot
be denied to the present appellants on the mere ground that the
present proceedings pertaining to a different project. After all, both the
acquisition proceedings are for the purpose of irrigation projects.
Therefore, basing on Ex.A6, this Court is inclined to enhance the
market value by two more times than the value fixed by the L.A.O.
8. In the result, the appeal is allowed enhancing the market value
by two more times (1+2) than the value fixed by the L.A.O. with all
other consequential benefits as stipulated under the amended Act.
There shall be no order as to'costs.
Pending miscellaneous applications, if any, shall stand closed.
JUSTICE G. SRI DEVI
JUSTICE M.G. PRTYN)ARSINI
15.o9.2022

and of GP for Appeals for the Respondent.

5. That there shall be no order as to costs in this appeal.
